Neural Tangents is a library for working with infinite-width neural networks, providing a high-level API for complex architectures and exact inference via Bayes' rule or gradient descent, with automatic distribution over multiple accelerators.

-----
978
Researchers address the issue of posterior collapse in variational autoencoders (VAEs) by introducing a new objective function that replaces KL divergence with a maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) term and a latent clipping technique.

2162
Researchers found that the generalization performance of linear graph embedding methods isn't due to the dimensionality constraint, but rather the small norm of the resulting vectors, supported by both theoretical and empirical evidence.

A new model, Sparse Deep Predictive Coding (SDPC), is introduced to assess the impact of inter-layer feedback connections on hierarchical sparse coding. SDPC outperforms a Hierarchical Lasso network in prediction error, convergence speed, and learning process.

This research introduces a novel ranking loss function for Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) in session-based recommendations, achieving improved performance of up to 51% over classical collaborative filtering and 35% over previous RNN solutions.

A novel end-to-end approach for unsupervised clustering is introduced, utilizing a differentiable loss function that optimizes normalized cuts for intra-cluster similarity and inter-cluster dissimilarity, achieving state-of-the-art results on various benchmarks.

Researchers applied deep neural networks (DNNs) to three cybersecurity tasks: Android malware classification, incident detection, and fraud detection. DNNs outperformed classical machine learning algorithms, achieving high accuracy rates of up to 0.972 in these tasks.

Researchers introduced the NeuroFovea metamer model, a foveated generative model that creates perceptually indistinguishable yet physically different images using a mixture of peripheral representations and style transfer algorithms.

Researchers propose a new framework, Wasserstein-Bounded GAN (WBGAN), which improves the stability of Wasserstein GANs by adding an upper-bound constraint to the Wasserstein term, enabling faster and more stable training.

This research paper introduces the Information Maximising Autoencoder (InfoAE), a model that learns a disentangled representation from unlabelled data by maximising the mutual information between the representation and given information in an unsupervised manner.

Researchers propose using Invertible Neural Networks (INNs) to determine hidden system parameters from measurements by learning the forward process and capturing lost information through latent variables.

1287
A new algorithm, the augment-REINFORCE-merge (ARM) estimator, is proposed for efficient backpropagation of gradients through stochastic binary layers. It combines variable augmentation, REINFORCE, and reparameterization to reduce variance in Monte Carlo integration and achieve state-of-the-art performance in auto-encoding variational inference and maximum likelihood estimation.

We propose Generative Adversarial Privacy and Fairness (GAPF), a data-driven framework for creating private and fair data representations by leveraging adversarial learning to decouple sensitive attributes from the rest of the data.

Here is a summary of the abstract in about 30 words:

The augment-REINFORCE-merge (ARM) estimator is proposed, providing an unbiased, low-variance, and computationally efficient method for backpropagating gradients through stochastic binary layers in discrete latent variable models.

Researchers propose two methods - Trace-norm regression (TNR) and Stable Trace-norm Analysis (StaTNA) - to enhance recommender systems with side information, outperforming state-of-the-art models on MovieLens datasets.
